+      const ch = H.ch; const to = setTimeout(() => { ch.kill(); H = null; res('一轮超过 5 分钟，强制停止'); }, 300e3);
+      const on = async (m) => { if (m.t === 'log') api.log(m.m); else if (m.t === 'notify') api.notify(m.m); else if (m.t === 'panel') savePanel(id, L.m.meta, m.p);
+        else if (m.t === 'call') { const fn = { price: api.price, buy: api.buy, sell: api.sell, call: api.call, position: api.position, spentToday: api.spentToday, fetchJson: readData, read: readChain, balances: readBal }[m.name]; let v, err; try { if (!fn) throw new Error('没有这个功能'); v = await fn(...(Array.isArray(m.args) ? m.args.slice(0, 3) : [])); } catch (e) { err = e.message; } if (ch.connected) ch.send({ t: 'ret', id: m.id, v, err }); }
+        else if (m.t === 'done') { clearTimeout(to); ch.off('message', on); if (m.state && typeof m.state === 'object') { st.state = m.state; } res(m.err || ''); } };
+      ch.on('message', on); ch.send({ t: 'tick', id, settings: conf(), state: st.state }); });
+    setInterval(async () => { if (busy || !conf().on) return; if (L.item.trades && ctx.walletBusy?.()) return; /* 只看不买的外挂（面板）不用等钱包空下来 */ busy = true; try { if (!H?.ch?.connected) { H = await spawnHost(join(PD, L.f)).catch(() => null); } const e = await runTick(); if (e) log(`[外挂 ${id}] ${e}`); save(); } catch (e) { log(`[外挂 ${id}] 出错：${e.message}`); } finally { busy = false; } }, every).unref();
+    log(`外挂 ${L.f}（${id}）已载入`);
+  }
+  globalThis.PLUG_RESERVE_FN = () => { let r = 0; for (const L of loaded) { const id = L.m.meta.id; const z = radarSet(); if (!Number(z[`plug.${id}.on`])) continue; const cap = Number(z[`plug.${id}.dayUsd`] ?? L.m.meta.dayUsd ?? 20) || 0; r += Math.max(0, cap - ((S.plug[id]?.spent || {})[day()] || 0)); } r = Math.min(r, Math.max(0, allCap() - allSpent())); for (const p of Object.values(S.plug || {})) { const q = p.pos?.[STABLES.USDT]?.qty || 0; if (q > 0) r += q; } return r; }; /* 外挂今天还能花的＋外挂持有的 USDT，雷达都不碰 */
+  globalThis.PLUG_HELD = (addr) => { let q = 0; const k = String(addr).toLowerCase(); for (const p of Object.values(S.plug || {})) q += p.pos?.[k]?.qty || 0; return q; }; /* 雷达卖出时扣掉外挂的部分 */ /* 外挂今天还能花的 USDT，雷达不会拿去用 */
+  return { found };
+}
diff --git "a/plugins/\345\256\232\346\212\225.mjs" "b/plugins/\345\256\232\346\212\225.mjs"
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1e0d036
--- /dev/null
+++ "b/plugins/\345\256\232\346\212\225.mjs"
@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+// 定投 DCA（官方范本）：每隔固定时间买一点，不猜高低，摊平成本。
+// 本金是钱包里的 USDT；只会买，不会自动卖（要卖用网格或自己手动）。
+export const meta = {
+  id: 'dca', name: '定投', nameEn: 'DCA', everySec: 300, dayUsd: 20,
+  desc: '每隔一段时间固定买一点大币，摊平成本。', descEn: 'Buy a fixed amount of a blue chip on a schedule.',
+  settings: [
+    { k: 'coin', def: 0, min: 0, max: 1, zh: '币种（0=BNB，1=ETH）', en: 'Coin (0=BNB, 1=ETH)', dzh: '只开放大币', den: 'Blue chips only' },
+    { k: 'everyH', def: 24, min: 1, max: 720, unit: '小时', uen: 'h', zh: '每隔几小时买一次', en: 'Every (hours)', dzh: '', den: '' },
+    { k: 'usd', def: 5, min: 1, max: 1e9, unit: 'U', zh: '每次买多少', en: 'Amount', dzh: '', den: '' },
+  ],
+};
+export async function tick(api) {
+  const c = api.settings(); const coin = c.coin >= 1 ? 'ETH' : 'BNB'; const s = api.state;
+  if (s.last && Date.now() - s.last < c.everyH * 3600e3) return;
+  const r = await api.buy(coin, c.usd); if (!r.ok) { if (!s.warned || Date.now() - s.warned > 6 * 3600e3) { s.warned = Date.now(); api.log('定投没买成：' + r.why); } return; }
+  s.last = Date.now(); api.notify(`定投买进 ${coin} ${c.usd}U`);
+}
diff --git "a/plugins/\347\275\221\346\240\274.mjs" "b/plugins/\347\275\221\346\240\274.mjs"
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9ec0f11
--- /dev/null
+++ "b/plugins/\347\275\221\346\240\274.mjs"
@@ -0,0 +1,32 @@
+// 网格交易（官方范本）：价格在区间内每跌一格买一份、每涨一格卖一份，赚来回的价差。
+// 只适合 BNB、ETH 这种大币；土狗不要用网格（一天跌 90% 会整个套住）。
+// 本金是钱包里的 USDT；只卖网格自己买的部位，碰不到雷达打到的币。
+export const meta = {
+  id: 'grid', name: '网格交易', nameEn: 'Grid trading', everySec: 60, dayUsd: 50,
+  desc: '在价格区间内低买高卖。只用于大币，本金是 USDT。', descEn: 'Buy low / sell high inside a range. Blue chips only; funded with USDT.',
+  settings: [
+    { k: 'coin', def: 0, min: 0, max: 1, zh: '币种（0=BNB，1=ETH）', en: 'Coin (0=BNB, 1=ETH)', dzh: '只开放大币', den: 'Blue chips only' },
+    { k: 'low', def: 0, min: 0, max: 100000, unit: 'U', zh: '区间下限', en: 'Range low', dzh: '0 = 自动（开始时的现价往下 15%）。跌破就不再买', den: '0 = auto (start price −15%). No buys below' },
+    { k: 'high', def: 0, min: 0, max: 100000, unit: 'U', zh: '区间上限', en: 'Range high', dzh: '0 = 自动（开始时的现价往上 15%）。涨破就不再卖', den: '0 = auto (start price +15%). No sells above' },
+    { k: 'levels', def: 10, min: 2, max: 50, unit: '格', uen: 'levels', zh: '分几格', en: 'Levels', dzh: '格子越多，交易越频繁、每次赚越少', den: 'More levels = more, smaller trades' },
+    { k: 'perUsd', def: 5, min: 1, max: 1e9, unit: 'U', zh: '每格买多少', en: 'Per level', dzh: '每跌一格买进的金额', den: 'Buy size per level' },
+  ],
+};
+export async function tick(api) {
+  const c = api.settings(); const coin = c.coin >= 1 ? 'ETH' : 'BNB';
+  const px = await api.price(coin); const s0 = api.state;
+  if (s0.coin !== coin || !(s0.autoLow > 0)) { s0.autoLow = px * 0.85; s0.autoHigh = px * 1.15; }
+  const low = c.low > 0 ? c.low : s0.autoLow, high = c.high > 0 ? c.high : s0.autoHigh; c.low = low; c.high = high;
+  if (!(c.high > c.low)) return api.log('区间设定不对：上限要大于下限');
+  const step = (c.high - c.low) / c.levels;
+  const lv = Math.max(0, Math.min(c.levels, Math.floor((px - c.low) / step)));
+  const s = api.state; if (s.coin !== coin) { s.coin = coin; s.lv = lv; s.lots = []; api.log(`开始：${coin} 现价 ${px.toFixed(2)}，区间 ${c.low.toFixed(2)}～${c.high.toFixed(2)}，在第 ${lv} 格`); api.notify(`网格开始：${coin} 区间 ${c.low.toFixed(0)}～${c.high.toFixed(0)}，分 ${c.levels} 格，每格 ${c.perUsd}U`); return; }
+  if (px < c.low || px > c.high) return; // 区间外不动作
+  if (lv < s.lv) { // 跌了 → 每跌一格买一份
+    for (let i = 0; i < s.lv - lv; i++) { const r = await api.buy(coin, c.perUsd); if (!r.ok) { api.log('买不了：' + r.why); break; } s.lots.push({ qty: r.qty, at: px }); api.notify(`${coin} 跌到 ${px.toFixed(2)}，买进 ${c.perUsd}U`); }
+  } else if (lv > s.lv) { // 涨了 → 每涨一格卖一份（先卖最便宜买的）
+    s.lots.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at);
+    for (let i = 0; i < lv - s.lv && s.lots.length; i++) { const lot = s.lots[0]; if (px < lot.at + step * 0.9) break; const r = await api.sell(coin, lot.qty); if (!r.ok) { api.log('卖不了：' + r.why); break; } s.lots.shift(); api.notify(`${coin} 涨到 ${px.toFixed(2)}，卖出一份，回收 ${r.usd.toFixed(2)}U`); }
+  }
+  s.lv = lv;
+}
diff --git "a/plugins/\351\222\261\345\214\205\346\200\273\350\247\210.mjs" "b/plugins/\351\222\261\345\214\205\346\200\273\350\247\210.mjs"
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ada6b48
--- /dev/null
+++ "b/plugins/\351\222\261\345\214\205\346\200\273\350\247\210.mjs"
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+// 钱包总览（官方范本，只看不买）：两条链的余额一次看完，钱不够打下一笔会提醒你。
+// 每分钟更新，显示在雷达「🧩 我的面板」。想加别的资料？叫你的 AI 照这个档案改（api.balances 读余额、api.fetchJson 读公开行情、api.panel 交给雷达显示）。
+export const meta = {
+  id: 'wallets', name: '钱包总览', nameEn: 'Wallet overview', everySec: 60, dayUsd: 0,
+  desc: '只看不买：每个钱包在 BSC、Robinhood 的余额合计，不够打下一笔会提醒。', descEn: 'View only: balances on BSC and Robinhood per wallet, with low-balance warnings.',
+  settings: [
+    { k: 'warnBsc', def: 15, min: 0, max: 1e9, unit: 'U', zh: 'BSC 余额低于多少提醒', en: 'Warn if BSC balance below', dzh: 'BNB＋USDT＋USDC 合计', den: 'BNB + USDT + USDC total' },
+    { k: 'warnRh', def: 10, min: 0, max: 1e9, unit: 'U', zh: 'Robinhood ETH 低于多少提醒', en: 'Warn if Robinhood ETH below', dzh: '', den: '' },
+  ],
+};
+export async function tick(api) {
+  const c = api.settings(); const b = await api.balances(); const items = [];
+  if (!b || !Array.isArray(b.w) || !b.w.length) { api.panel({ title: '钱包总览', items: [{ type: 'alert', text: '还读不到钱包余额：请先在「设定 → 钱包」登入，雷达开着约 1 分钟后就会出现。', tone: 'warn' }] }); return; }
+  const bp = Number(b.bnbUsd) || 0, ep = Number(b.ethUsd) || 0; const f = (v, d) => (Number(v) || 0).toFixed(d);
+  let tb = 0, tr = 0; const rows = [];
+  for (const w of b.w) { const bsc = (w.bsc?.BNB || 0) * bp + (w.bsc?.USDT || 0) + (w.bsc?.USDC || 0); const rh = (w.rh?.ETH || 0) * ep; tb += bsc; tr += rh;
+    rows.push([w.name, f(w.bsc?.BNB, 4), f(w.bsc?.USDT, 2), f(w.bsc?.USDC, 2), f(w.rh?.ETH, 5), '$' + f(bsc + rh, 1)]);
+    if (bsc < c.warnBsc) items.push({ type: 'alert', text: `[${w.name}] BSC 只剩约 ${f(bsc, 1)}U，低于你设的 ${c.warnBsc}U。不够的话叫你的 AI 换一些 BNB 进来（手续费也要 BNB）。`, tone: 'dn' });
+    if (w.rh?.ETH != null && rh < c.warnRh) items.push({ type: 'alert', text: `[${w.name}] Robinhood 链上 ETH 约 ${f(rh, 1)}U，低于 ${c.warnRh}U。雷达不能帮你跨链，要用的话叫你的 AI 把 BNB 跨到 Robinhood 换成 ETH。`, tone: 'dn' }); }
+  items.unshift({ type: 'stat', label: '全部合计', value: '$' + f(tb + tr, 1), sub: `${b.w.length} 个钱包` }, { type: 'stat', label: 'BSC', value: '$' + f(tb, 1), sub: `BNB $${f(bp, 0)}` }, { type: 'stat', label: 'Robinhood', value: '$' + f(tr, 1), sub: `ETH $${f(ep, 0)}` });
+  items.push({ type: 'table', cols: ['钱包', 'BNB', 'USDT', 'USDC', 'ETH（Robinhood）', '合计'], rows });
+  const s = api.state; s.h = Array.isArray(s.h) ? s.h : []; s.h.push([Date.now(), +(tb + tr).toFixed(2)]); if (s.h.length > 1440) s.h.splice(0, s.h.length - 1440);
+  if (s.h.length > 1) items.push({ type: 'line', label: '钱包总值走势（美元）', points: s.h });
+  api.panel({ title: '钱包总览', items });
+}
diff --git a/preload.cjs b/preload.cjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1efbeae
--- /dev/null
+++ b/preload.cjs
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');
+contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('radar', {
+  snapshot: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('snapshot'),
+  pools: (n) => ipcRenderer.invoke('pools', n),
+  schema: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('schema'),
+  login: (i) => ipcRenderer.invoke('login', i),
+  copyGuide: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('copyGuide'),
+  plugins: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('plugins'),
+  journal: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('journal'),
+  donate: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('donate'),
+  copyText: (t) => ipcRenderer.invoke('copyText', t),
+  checkToken: (a) => ipcRenderer.invoke('checkToken', a),
+  exportJournal: (rows) => ipcRenderer.invoke('exportJournal', rows),
+  approvePlugin: (f, h) => ipcRenderer.invoke('approvePlugin', f, h),
+  saveSettings: (s) => ipcRenderer.invoke('saveSettings', s),
+  pause: (on) => ipcRenderer.invoke('pause', on),
+  order: (o) => ipcRenderer.invoke('order', o),
+  open: (u) => ipcRenderer.invoke('open', u),
+  notify: (t, b) => ipcRenderer.invoke('notify', t, b),
+});
diff --git a/radar/main.cjs b/radar/main.cjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..abb906a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/radar/main.cjs
@@ -0,0 +1,179 @@
+// 罗宾猫雷达 · 桌面程式主程序
+const { app, BrowserWindow, Tray, Menu, ipcMain, nativeImage, Notification, shell, clipboard, dialog } = require('electron');
+let QR = null; try { QR = require('qrcode'); } catch {}
+const qrCache = { url: '', img: '' };
+const path = require('path'), fs = require('fs');
+const ROOT = path.join(__dirname, '..');               // 打狗工具资料夹（GangPaper）
+const DATA = path.join(ROOT, 'data');
+const SET = path.join(DATA, 'radar_settings.json');
+const ICON = path.join(__dirname, 'ui', 'logo.png');
+let win = null, tray = null, quitting = false;
+
+if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) { app.quit(); }
+app.on('second-instance', () => { if (win) { win.show(); win.focus(); } });
+
+function readJSON(f, d) { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8')); } catch { return d; } }
+function readText(f) { try { return fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'); } catch { return ''; } }
+
+// 从打狗工具的状态档整理出看盘 / 持仓 / 状态
+let cache = { at: 0, v: null };
+function snapshot() {
+  if (Date.now() - cache.at < 3000 && cache.v) return cache.v;
+  const S = readJSON(path.join(DATA, 'watch_state.json'), {});
+  const now = Date.now(), px = S.lastBnbUsd || 0;
+  const board = Object.entries(S.paid || {}).filter(([c, P]) => now - (P.at || 0) < 172800e3).map(([c, P]) => {
+    const prog = Math.round((P.progress || 0) * 100), ag = P.agents || 0, grp = Object.keys(S.grpHits?.[c]?.chats || {}).length;
+    const done = Object.values(P.done || {}).some((x) => x === 'minted' || x === 'free');
+    const why = String(P.lastReason || '');
+    let verdict = 'watch', note = `进度要 60%、AI 钱包要 100 个`;
+    if (done) { verdict = 'done'; note = '已打'; }
+    else if (/不打|騙|贔|貔貅|大量部署|黑名單|不是 Agent/.test(why)) { verdict = 'no'; note = why.replace(/，?照規則不打|，先不打.*$/, ''); }
+    else if (prog >= 60 && ag >= 100) { verdict = 'ok'; note = '符合条件'; }
+    return { c, name: P.name || c.slice(0, 8), prog, ag, grp, verdict, note, at: P.at || 0, col: done ? 'done' : prog >= 30 ? 'hot' : 'new' };
+  }).filter((x) => x.verdict !== 'no' || x.grp > 0 || x.ag > 20).sort((a, b) => (b.ag + b.prog * 3 + b.grp * 30) - (a.ag + a.prog * 3 + a.grp * 30)).filter((x, i) => i < 90 || x.verdict === 'done');
+  const hold = (S.dyn || []).filter((t) => !t.closed).map((t) => {
+    const keyMe = Object.keys(S.tokens || {}).find((k) => k === t.sym || k.endsWith(':' + t.sym));
+    const st = S.tokens?.[keyMe] || {};
+    const cv = S.curveVal?.[t.addr + ':' + t.only]; return { sym: t.sym, addr: t.addr, cost: t.cost || 0, value: st.last?.v ?? (cv != null ? cv : null), curveOk: cv != null, done: !!st.done, stage: st.stage || 0, wallet: t.only };
+  }).filter((h) => !h.done).slice(-40).reverse();
+  for (const [c, P] of Object.entries(S.rh?.pos || {})) { const ws = Object.values(P.w || {}).filter((w) => !w.closed); if (!ws.length) continue; hold.unshift({ sym: P.sym, addr: c, chain: 'rh', cost: ws.reduce((a, w) => a + (w.cost || 0), 0), value: ws.some((w) => w.val != null) ? ws.reduce((a, w) => a + (w.val || 0), 0) : null, stage: Math.min(...ws.map((w) => w.stage || 0)) }); } /* Robinhood 持仓 */
+  const hb = readText(path.join(DATA, 'watch_heartbeat.txt'));
+  const login = readJSON(path.join(DATA, 'radar_login.json'), null); if (login && Date.now() - (login.at || 0) > 600e3) login.stale = 1;
+  if (login?.state === 'qr' && login.url && qrCache.url === login.url) login.img = qrCache.img;
+  const rhMint = Object.entries(S.rh?.mint || {}).filter(([, M]) => M.recent != null || Object.keys(M.m || {}).length >= 10).map(([c, M]) => ({ c, name: M.name || '', sym: M.sym || '', nft: !!M.nft, total: Object.keys(M.m || {}).length, recent: M.recent ?? null, real: M.real ?? null, fresh: M.freshPct ?? null, price: M.priceUsd ?? null, cost: M.costUsd ?? null, ok: !!M.ok, why: M.why || '还没检查', done: !!(M.done && Object.keys(M.done).length), age: Math.round((Date.now() - (M.first || Date.now())) / 60e3) })).sort((a, b) => (b.real || 0) - (a.real || 0) || b.total - a.total).slice(0, 60);
+  const smart = { related: Object.entries(S.smartW?.related || {}).map(([a, r]) => ({ a, of: r.of, ch: r.ch })), events: (S.smartW?.events || []).slice(-30).reverse() };
+  const bscNft = Object.entries(S.bscNft?.m || {}).filter(([, M]) => Object.keys(M.m || {}).length >= 10).map(([c, M]) => ({ c, name: M.name || '', sym: M.sym || '', nft: true, total: Object.keys(M.m || {}).length, recent: M.recent ?? null, real: M.real ?? null, fresh: M.freshPct ?? null, cost: M.costUsd ?? null, ok: !!M.ok, why: M.why || '还没检查', done: !!(M.done && Object.keys(M.done).length), age: Math.round((Date.now() - (M.first || Date.now())) / 60e3) })).sort((a, b) => (b.real || 0) - (a.real || 0) || b.total - a.total).slice(0, 60);
+  recHist(board, rhMint, bscNft);
+  const lookup = readJSON(path.join(DATA, 'radar_lookup.json'), null); const bal = readJSON(path.join(DATA, 'radar_bal.json'), null); const anal = readJSON(path.join(DATA, 'radar_analyze.json'), null), mhist = readJSON(path.join(DATA, 'radar_minthist.json'), null); const panels = (() => { try { const d = path.join(DATA, 'panels'); return fs.existsSync(d) ? fs.readdirSync(d).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.json')).slice(0, 30).map((f) => readJSON(path.join(d, f), null)).filter(Boolean) : []; } catch { return []; } })();
+  const v = { board, hold, hb, bnbUsd: px, rhMint, bscNft, smart, lookup, bal, panels, anal, mhist, official: officialCheck(), set: readJSON(SET, {}), paused: fs.existsSync(path.join(DATA, 'pause_buy.txt')), wallets: readJSON(path.join(DATA, 'radar_wallets.json'), { list: [] }).list || [], login };
+  cache = { at: Date.now(), v }; return v;
+}
+
+ipcMain.handle('snapshot', async () => { const v = snapshot(); const L = v.login; if (L?.state === 'qr' && L.url && /^https:\/\/app\.binance\.com\//.test(L.url) && qrCache.url !== L.url && QR) { qrCache.url = L.url; qrCache.img = await QR.toDataURL(L.url, { margin: 1, width: 220 }).catch(() => ''); L.img = qrCache.img; } return v; });
+ipcMain.handle('login', (e, i) => { try { fs.unlinkSync(path.join(DATA, 'radar_login.json')); } catch {} fs.writeFileSync(path.join(DATA, 'radar_login_req.json'), JSON.stringify({ i: Number(i) || 0, at: Date.now() })); cache.at = 0; return true; });

+function createWindow() {

+// 程式更新后自动套用：介面档改了就重新整理，主程式改了就自己重开（不用玩家手动重启）
+let rlT = null; const watchFile = (f, full) => { try { fs.watchFile(f, { interval: 3000 }, (cur, prev) => { if (cur.mtimeMs === prev.mtimeMs) return; clearTimeout(rlT); rlT = setTimeout(() => { if (full) { app.relaunch(); quitting = true; app.exit(0); } else if (win) win.webContents.reloadIgnoringCache(); }, 1500); }); } catch {} }; /* 用定时比对修改时间（fs.watch 在共享资料夹收不到通知，改了不会更新） */
+app.whenReady().then(() => { watchFile(path.join(__dirname, 'ui', 'index.html'), false); watchFile(path.join(__dirname, 'main.cjs'), true); watchFile(path.join(__dirname, 'preload.cjs'), true); watchFile(path.join(__dirname, 'settings_schema.json'), false); });
+app.on('window-all-closed', (e) => e.preventDefault?.());
+

+{
+  "name": "robincat-radar",
+  "productName": "罗宾猫雷达",
+  "version": "3.0.0",
+  "description": "罗宾猫雷达 — BSC 与 Robinhood 链的 AI 热门项目雷达",
+  "main": "main.cjs",
+  "private": true,
+  "dependencies": {
+    "electron": "^33.4.11",
+    "qrcode": "^1.5.4"
+  }
+}
diff --git a/radar/preload.cjs b/radar/preload.cjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3ba16bf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/radar/preload.cjs
@@ -0,0 +1,27 @@
+const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');
+contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('radar', {
+  snapshot: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('snapshot'),
+  pools: (n) => ipcRenderer.invoke('pools', n),
+  schema: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('schema'),
+  login: (i) => ipcRenderer.invoke('login', i),
+  copyGuide: (x) => ipcRenderer.invoke('copyGuide', x),
+  plugins: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('plugins'),
+  journal: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('journal'),
+  donate: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('donate'),
+  copyText: (t) => ipcRenderer.invoke('copyText', t),
+  checkToken: (a) => ipcRenderer.invoke('checkToken', a),
+  tokenDetail: (n, a) => ipcRenderer.invoke('tokenDetail', n, a),
+  hist: (k) => ipcRenderer.invoke('hist', k),
+  feedback: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('feedback'),
+  tgChannels: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('tgChannels'),
+  tgChannelsSave: (l) => ipcRenderer.invoke('tgChannelsSave', l),
+  fbDone: (id, v) => ipcRenderer.invoke('fbDone', id, v),
+  exportJournal: (rows) => ipcRenderer.invoke('exportJournal', rows),
+  approvePlugin: (f, h) => ipcRenderer.invoke('approvePlugin', f, h),
+  saveSettings: (s) => ipcRenderer.invoke('saveSettings', s),
+  importAI: (k, n, b) => ipcRenderer.invoke('importAI', k, n, b),
+  pause: (on) => ipcRenderer.invoke('pause', on),
+  order: (o) => ipcRenderer.invoke('order', o),
+  open: (u) => ipcRenderer.invoke('open', u),
+  notify: (t, b) => ipcRenderer.invoke('notify', t, b),
+});

+{
+ "_说明": "罗宾猫雷达的所有可调数字。玩家在「设定」里改，或叫自己的 AI 改 data/radar_settings.json。新增设定：在这里加一项（k 不能重复），再到 watch.mjs 的 applySettings() 或 rh.mjs 的 applyRH() 接上。",

+   "en": "Robinhood Chain · Mint projects"
+  },

+   "id": "rhsell",

+   "en": "Robinhood Chain · Sell & protection"
+  }
+ ],
+ "items": [
+  {

+   "g": "bsc",
+   "type": "bool",
+   "def": 1,
+   "min": 0,
+   "max": 1,
+   "zh": "自动铸造",

+   "den": "Off = BSC AI mint projects are alerts only"

+  {
+   "k": "bsc.mintUsd",
+   "g": "bsc",
+   "def": 20,
+   "min": 1,
+   "max": 1000000000.0,
+   "unit": "U",
+   "zh": "每个项目每笔最多",
+   "en": "Max per project",
+   "dzh": "每个钱包每个铸造项目最多花多少",
+   "den": "Max spend per wallet per mint project"
+  },
+  {
+   "k": "bsc.mintDayUsd",
+   "g": "bsc",
+   "def": 60,
+   "min": 5,
+   "max": 1000000000.0,
+   "unit": "U",
+   "zh": "每天最多",
+   "en": "Daily cap",
+   "dzh": "每个钱包每天花在铸造项目的总上限",
+   "den": "Daily total per wallet on mint projects"
+  },
+  {
+   "k": "bsc.goPct",
+   "g": "bsc",
+   "def": 60,
+   "min": 10,
+   "max": 100,
+   "unit": "%",
+   "zh": "铸造进度要到",
+   "en": "Mint progress needed",
+   "dzh": "进度到这里才打（太早容易被项目方钓鱼）",
+   "den": "Only mint after progress reaches this"
+  },
+  {
+   "k": "bsc.agMin",
+   "g": "bsc",
+   "def": 100,
+   "min": 20,
+   "max": 2000,
+   "unit": "个",
+   "uen": "wallets",
+   "zh": "AI 钱包至少",

+   "den": "Agent ID (KYC) wallets minting before we join"

+  {

+   "zh": "捆绑钱包最多",

+   "den": "Skip if wallets that bought together at launch / fresh / identical-size buys hold more than this"
+  },

+   "min": 0,

+   "den": "Skip if the top 10 holders (excluding the pool) hold more than this"

+   "def": 60,

+   "zh": "回本后回落全卖",

diff --git a/radar/ui/index.html b/radar/ui/index.html
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3f703d0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/radar/ui/index.html
@@ -0,0 +1,539 @@
+<!DOCTYPE html>
+<html lang="zh-CN">
+<head>
+<meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'">
+<title>罗宾猫雷达</title>
+<style>
+:root{--bg:#0b0d11;--card:#151922;--card2:#1c212c;--line:#262c38;--ink:#eceff4;--mute:#8a93a5;--gold:#f0b90b;--red:#ff6161;--green:#2ecc8f;--purple:#a78bfa;--blue:#60a5fa}
+*{box-sizing:border-box}
+body{margin:0;min-width:1100px;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:14px/1.5 -apple-system,"PingFang SC","Microsoft YaHei",system-ui,sans-serif}
+header{height:56px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:22px;padding:0 22px;background:#07090c;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:55}body{padding-top:56px}#offb{position:sticky;top:56px;z-index:54}
+.brand{white-space:nowrap;flex:none;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-weight:700;font-size:16px}
+.lg{width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:9px}.lgb{width:48px;height:48px;border-radius:12px}.logo{width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:8px;background:conic-gradient(from 200deg,#f0b90b,#ffe07a,#b07d00,#f0b90b);box-shadow:0 0 14px #f0b90b55;position:relative}.logo:after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:6px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid #0009}
+.tabs{display:flex;gap:4px}.tabs a{padding:8px 12px;border-radius:9px;color:var(--mute);cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap}.tabs a.on{background:var(--card2);color:var(--ink)}
+.right{margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex:none}.right .btn,.right .status{white-space:nowrap}
+.status{white-space:nowrap;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:13px}
+.status i{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--blue);box-shadow:0 0 6px var(--blue)}.status.auto i{background:var(--green);box-shadow:0 0 6px var(--green)}
+.btn{border:0;border-radius:9px;padding:8px 14px;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit}
+.btn.y{background:var(--gold);color:#111}.btn.g{background:var(--card2);color:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--line)}.btn.r{background:#3a1d1d;color:var(--red)}.btn.s{padding:6px 11px;font-size:12px}
+main{padding:20px 22px}.page{display:none}.page.on{display:block}
+.filters{display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:14px}.f{padding:6px 14px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--mute);cursor:pointer;font-size:13px}.f.on{background:var(--gold);color:#111;border-color:var(--gold)}
+.cols{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:16px}.cols .col>div{max-height:calc(100vh - 300px);overflow:auto;padding-right:4px}#flat{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(330px,1fr));gap:14px}
+.col h3{margin:0 0 10px;font-size:14px;color:var(--mute)}
+.tk{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:6px 10px;align-items:center}
+.tk.go{border-color:#2b6a4c}
+.tk b{font-size:14.5px}.tk small{color:var(--mute);font-size:12px}
+.bar{grid-column:1/3;height:5px;background:var(--card2);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden}.bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:var(--gold)}
+.meta{grid-column:1/3;display:flex;gap:14px;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--mute)}.meta span b{color:var(--ink)}
+.ai{color:var(--purple)}
+.v{font-size:12px;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:7px;white-space:nowrap}.v.ok{background:#12301f;color:var(--green)}.v.wt{background:#33290f;color:var(--gold)}.v.no{background:#341a1a;color:var(--red)}
+table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden}
+th,td{padding:12px 14px;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}th{color:var(--mute);font-weight:500;font-size:12.5px;background:var(--card2)}
+.up{color:var(--green)}.dn{color:var(--red)}
+.sum{display:flex;gap:14px;margin-bottom:14px}.sum div{flex:1;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:14px}.sum small{color:var(--mute)}.sum b{display:block;font-size:22px}
+.set{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:16px;max-width:1240px}
+.box{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:18px}
+.box h3{margin:0 0 12px;font-size:15px}
+input[type=date]{background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink);border-radius:8px;padding:6px 8px;color-scheme:dark}
+select{background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink);border-radius:8px;padding:7px 9px;font-family:inherit}
+.ch{display:inline-block;padding:6px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:999px;margin-right:6px;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px}.ch.on{border-color:var(--gold);background:#1f1a0b}
+.styles{display:flex;gap:10px}.st{flex:1;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:11px;padding:12px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer}.st small{display:block;color:var(--mute);font-size:12px}.st.on{border-color:var(--gold);background:#1f1a0b}
+.line{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}.line:first-of-type{border-top:0}
+.line label{flex:1}.line small{display:block;color:var(--mute);font-size:12px}
+input[type=number]{width:90px;background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--ink);border-radius:8px;padding:7px 9px;text-align:right;font-family:inherit;font-size:14px}
+.sw,.line label.sw{position:relative;width:44px;min-width:44px;max-width:44px;height:25px;flex:none;display:inline-block;padding:0;margin:0}.sw input{display:none}.sw i{position:absolute;inset:0;background:#333a48;border-radius:13px;transition:.2s}.sw i:after{content:"";position:absolute;width:19px;height:19px;left:3px;top:3px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;transition:.2s}.sw input:checked+i{background:var(--green)}.sw input:checked+i:after{left:22px}
+.adv{grid-column:1/3;text-align:center;color:var(--mute);cursor:pointer;padding:10px;border:1px dashed var(--line);border-radius:12px}
+.more{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:16px}
+.card{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:18px}
+.card h3{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:15px}.card p{color:var(--mute);margin:0 0 12px;font-size:13px}
+.cmd{font-family:ui-monospace,Consolas,monospace;background:var(--card2);border-radius:8px;padding:7px 10px;margin-bottom:6px;font-size:12.5px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between}.cmd span{color:var(--mute);font-family:inherit}
+textarea{width:100%;min-height:80px;background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:9px;color:var(--ink);padding:9px;font-family:inherit;resize:none}
+.link{font-family:ui-monospace,Consolas,monospace;background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:12.5px;margin-bottom:10px}
+.toast{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:24px;transform:translateX(-50%);background:#222838;border:1px solid var(--line);padding:10px 18px;border-radius:10px;display:none}
+#sres .v{display:inline-block;width:auto;padding:1px 8px;margin:0 4px 0 0;font-size:11px;vertical-align:1px}
+.lnk{cursor:pointer;border-bottom:1px dashed #fff3}.lnk:hover{color:var(--y,#f0b90b)}
+#dtl .wel{width:min(980px,94vw);max-height:90vh;overflow:auto;padding:22px 26px}
+.dg{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(130px,1fr));gap:8px;margin:12px 0}.dg>div{background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:9px 11px}.dg small{display:block;color:var(--mute);font-size:11.5px}.dg b{font-size:16px}
+.tf{display:flex;gap:6px;margin:6px 0}.tf span{flex:1;text-align:center;background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:6px;font-size:12px}
+.pst{background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:9px 11px}.pst small{display:block;color:var(--mute);font-size:11.5px}.pst b{font-size:17px}
+.seg{display:inline-flex;background:#0a0c10;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:3px;gap:2px}.seg a{padding:7px 16px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;color:var(--mute);user-select:none}.seg a.on{background:#f0b90b;color:#111;font-weight:700}.seg a:hover:not(.on){color:var(--ink)}
+.aitab{color:#f0b90b!important;font-weight:700}.aitab:not(.on)::after{content:"新";font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#111;background:#f0b90b;border-radius:6px;padding:0 4px;margin-left:5px;vertical-align:2px}.aihero{display:flex;gap:20px;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:22px 24px;border-radius:16px;border:1px solid #f0b90b55;background:linear-gradient(120deg,#f0b90b14,#8b7cf614)}.aiexs{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(300px,1fr));gap:10px}.aiex{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;padding:12px 14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:#0a0c10;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px}.aiex:hover{border-color:#f0b90b}.aiex b{margin-left:auto;color:#f0b90b;font-size:12.5px;white-space:nowrap}
+@media (max-width:1400px){header{gap:10px;padding:0 12px}.tabs a{padding:7px 8px;font-size:13.5px}.right{gap:6px}.right .btn,.right .status{padding:6px 9px;font-size:12.5px}.aitab:not(.on)::after{display:none}}@media (max-width:1080px){.brand{font-size:0;gap:0}.brand img{width:30px;height:30px}}
+[data-theme=light]{--bg:#f4f5f7;--card:#ffffff;--card2:#eef0f4;--line:#dde1e8;--ink:#1b1e25;--mute:#687083;--red:#e5484d;--green:#14975b}
+[data-theme=light] header{background:#ffffff!important}
+[data-theme=light] [style*="#0a0c10"],[data-theme=light] .seg,[data-theme=light] .aiex,[data-theme=light] .pst,[data-theme=light] .dg>div,[data-theme=light] .tf span{background:#f3f4f7!important}
+[data-theme=light] input,[data-theme=light] select,[data-theme=light] textarea{background:#fff!important;color:var(--ink)!important;border-color:var(--line)}
+[data-theme=light] .ov{background:#0007}
+[data-theme=light] .seg a.on,[data-theme=light] .btn.y{color:#111}
+[data-theme=light] #offb{filter:saturate(.8)}
+.ov{position:fixed;inset:0;background:#000c;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:50}
+.wel{width:620px;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;padding:24px 26px}
+.wel h2{margin:0 0 4px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}.wel .sub{color:var(--mute);margin-bottom:14px}
+.wel ul{margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none}.wel li{display:flex;gap:10px;padding:9px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line);font-size:13.5px}.wel li:first-child{border-top:0}.wel li b{color:var(--ink)}.wel li span{color:var(--mute)}
+.ok{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin:16px 0 12px;cursor:pointer}.ok input{width:17px;height:17px;accent-color:#f0b90b}
+.wel .btn[disabled]{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed}
+.steps li{align-items:flex-start}.n{flex:none;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--gold);color:#111;font-weight:700;font-size:12px;display:grid;place-items:center;margin-top:2px}
+.qr{display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:center;background:var(--card2);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-top:12px}.qrbox{width:92px;height:92px;background:#fff;color:#111;border-radius:8px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:18px;line-height:1.1;text-align:center;flex:none}
+</style>
+</head>
+<body>
+<header>
+  <div class="brand" style="cursor:pointer" title="回到看盘" onclick="document.querySelector('.tabs a[data-p=board]').click()"><img class="lg" src="logo.png">
+罗宾猫雷达</div>
+  <nav class="tabs"><a class="on" data-p="board">📡 看盘</a><a data-p="ai" class="aitab">✨ AI 加功能</a><a data-p="hold">💼 持仓</a><a data-p="set">⚙️ 设定</a><a data-p="more">📱 遥控与安全</a></nav>
+  <div class="right"><span class="status" id="offi" style="display:none;cursor:pointer" onclick="offiInfo()"></span><button class="btn g" onclick="openDonate()" style="color:#ff8fab">❤️ 打赏</button><button class="btn g" id="thm" onclick="toggleTheme()" title="白天／黑夜" style="min-width:40px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center" data-noi18n><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/><path d="M12 2v2M12 20v2M4.9 4.9l1.4 1.4M17.7 17.7l1.4 1.4M2 12h2M20 12h2M4.9 19.1l1.4-1.4M17.7 6.3l1.4-1.4"/></svg></button><button class="btn g" id="lang" onclick="setLang()" title="中文 / English" style="min-width:56px">EN</button><span class="status" style="color:var(--mute)" id="hb" title="雷达引擎最后一次回报的时间，每 5 秒自动更新，不用按">连线中…</span><span class="status auto" id="st" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="mode(!this.classList.contains('auto'))"><i></i><span id="stt">自动打 · 照推荐设定</span></span></div>
+</header>
+<div id="offb" style="display:none;background:#3a1d1d;color:#ffb4b4;padding:10px 22px;font-size:13px"></div>
+<main>
+
+<section class="page on" id="board">

+function TT(s){if(s==null)return s;if(L!=='en')return toS(s);const k=String(s).trim();if(DICT[k]!=null)return String(s).replace(k,DICT[k]);return String(s).replace(/^頻道項目$|^频道项目$/,'Channel project').replace(/^鑄造項目$|^铸造项目$/,'Mint project').replace(/进度要 (\d+)%、AI 钱包要 (\d+) 个/,'Needs $1% progress & $2 AI wallets').replace(/同一個部署者 (\S+?) ?24 ?小時內部署了 ?(\d+) ?個項目/,'Same deployer $1 launched $2 projects in 24h').replace(/(\d+) ?小时真实买家 (\d+) 个（要 (\d+) 个[^）]*）/,'$2 real buyers in $1h (need $3)').replace(/(\d+) 分钟内真人钱包 (\d+) 个（要 (\d+) 个）/,'$2 real minters in $1 min (need $3)').replace(/捆绑钱包持有 ([\d.]+)%（上限 (\d+)%）/,'Bundled wallets hold $1% (max $2%)').replace(/前 10 大持有 (\d+)%（上限 (\d+)%）/,'Top-10 hold $1% (max $2%)').replace(/部署者自己持有 ([\d.]+)%（上限 (\d+)%）/,'Deployer holds $1% (max $2%)').replace(/同名有好几个币（仿盘），跳过/,'Copycat: several tokens share this name').replace(/流动性 \$(\d+)，要 \$(\d+) 以上/,'Liquidity $$$1, need $$$2+').replace(/新开钱包占 (\d+)%（上限 (\d+)%[^）]*）/,'Fresh wallets $1% (max $2%)').replace(/找不到一般人自己铸造的交易（可能要白名单或签名）/,'No public self-mint found (allowlist/signature?)').replace(/一次(铸造要)?约 ([\d.]+)U（超过设定 ([\d.]+)U）/,'~$2U per mint (over your $3U)').replace(/^还没检查$/,'Not checked yet').replace(/^符合条件$/,'Eligible').replace(/^已打$/,'Bought').replace(/照規則不打|照规则不打/g,'skipped by rules').replace(/，?先不打.*$/,' — skipped for now').replace(/讀不到進度|读不到进度/,'progress unreadable').replace(/黑名單|黑名单/,'blacklist').replace(/貔貅/g,'honeypot').replace(/；/g,'; ').replace(/(\d+) 个 AI 钱包/,'$1 AI wallets').replace(/(\d+) 个群/,'$1 groups').replace(/(\d+) 分$/,'$1 min').replace(/(\d+) 小时$/,'$1 h').replace(/(\d+) 天$/,'$1 d')}
+function i18n(root){(root||document).querySelectorAll?.('[placeholder],[title]').forEach(el=>{if(el.dataset.noi18n)return;for(const at of ['placeholder','title']){const v=el.getAttribute(at);if(v==null)continue;if(el['__zh_'+at]==null){if(!/[\u4e00-\u9fff]/.test(v))continue;el['__zh_'+at]=v}el.setAttribute(at,L==='en'?(DICT[el['__zh_'+at]]??el['__zh_'+at]):el['__zh_'+at])}});const w=document.createTreeWalker(root||document.body,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT,{acceptNode:n=>n.parentNode&&/^(SCRIPT|STYLE|TEXTAREA)$/.test(n.parentNode.nodeName)?2:1});let n;while(n=w.nextNode()){if(n.__zh==null){if(!/[\u4e00-\u9fff]/.test(n.nodeValue))continue;n.__zh=n.nodeValue}n.nodeValue=L==='en'?TT(n.__zh):n.__zh}
+ (root||document).querySelectorAll('[placeholder]').forEach(e=>{if(e.__zh==null)e.__zh=e.placeholder;e.placeholder=L==='en'?TT(e.__zh):e.__zh});document.documentElement.lang=L==='en'?'en':'zh-CN';document.title=L==='en'?'RobinCat Radar':'罗宾猫雷达';const b=document.getElementById('lang');if(b)b.textContent=L==='en'?'中文':'EN'}
+function setLang(){L=L==='en'?'zh':'en';try{localStorage.setItem('lang',L)}catch{}try{renderCh()}catch{}if(SCH.length){renderSchema();renderRules();renderToks();renderSmart()}render();if(CH!=='mint')loadPools();i18n();try{renderOffi();paintMode();renderLogin();renderRhMint();renderTodo();renderRecent();renderPlugins();renderPanels();renderFb();applyTheme()}catch{}}

+function saveSet(msg,btn){{$$('#sch .bsh').forEach(h=>{h.textContent='';h.style.color=''});$$('#sch .bsv .btn').forEach(b=>b.style.boxShadow='');}const o={style:$('#set .st.on')?.textContent.trim().slice(2,4)};const scope=btn?.closest('.box')||$('#sch');scope.querySelectorAll('[data-k]').forEach(el=>{const it=SCH.find(x=>x.k===el.dataset.k);if(!it||/^plug\.[^.]+\.on$/.test(it.k))return;let v=it.type==='bool'?(el.checked?1:0):+el.value;if(!Number.isFinite(v))v=it.def;v=Math.min(it.max,Math.max(it.min,v));if(it.type!=='bool')el.value=v;o[it.k]=v===+it.def?null:v});/* 跟推荐值一样就不存：以后推荐值更新会自动套用 */const all={...(SNAP?.set||{})};for(const k in o)all[k]=o[k]??undefined;const cf=setConflicts(all);(async()=>{if(cf.length&&!await ask((L==='en'?'⚠️ These settings conflict':'⚠️ 这几个设定互相冲突')+'\n'+cf.map(x=>'⚠️ '+x).join('\n')+'\n'+(L==='en'?'Save anyway?':'还是要保存吗？（按取消回去改）')))return;await window.radar.saveSettings(o);t(msg);if(btn){const h=btn.parentElement.querySelector('.bsh');if(h){h.textContent=cf.length?(L==='en'?'Saved (with conflicts)':'已保存（有冲突）'):(L==='en'?'Saved ✓':'已保存 ✓');h.style.color=cf.length?'#f0b90b':'var(--green)'}}})()}

+const fU=(v)=>v==null||!isFinite(v)?'-':Math.abs(v)>=1e9?(v/1e9).toFixed(2)+'B':Math.abs(v)>=1e6?(v/1e6).toFixed(2)+'M':Math.abs(v)>=1e3?(v/1e3).toFixed(1)+'K':(+v).toFixed(v<1?2:0);

+const INFO={'捆绑':'开盘那几秒一起买、或是刚开的新钱包、或买一模一样数量的钱包，通常是同一个人（项目方）控制。比例高 = 容易被一次砸盘。','真人钱包':'交易过好几次、钱包里真的有钱的地址。刚开、没钱的分身钱包不算，比较难造假。','前10大':'持有最多的前 10 个地址（不算交易池）合计占多少。越高越集中，越容易被砸。','新开钱包':'几乎没交易过的新地址。比例太高，可能是项目方自己开很多钱包在刷热度。','曲线':'币还在发射平台（例如 four.meme、Aizo）上，用平台的价格曲线买卖，还没上 PancakeSwap，所以没有 K 线和市价。','AI钱包':'有 Binance Agent ID 的钱包（实名认证过的 AI 代理钱包），很难大量造假，所以拿来判断是不是真的有人在打。'};

+// 罗宾猫雷达 · Robinhood Chain（4663）自动打
+// 11/2 她定：两条链都要自动打，风险我自己抓、自己排除。Robinhood 没有 Agent ID，所以改用「真人买家＋防捆绑＋防抽池＋卖得掉」四层检查
+export const RHC = {
+  usd: 5, dayUsd: 30, maxPos: 3, minEth: 0.003,           // 每笔 10U、每个钱包每天最多 30U、同时最多持有 3 个
+  maxAgeMin: 120, minLiqUsd: 20000,          // 开池 2 小时内、流动性 ≥ 2 万美金
+  minRealBuyers: 80, oldNonce: 8, freshNonce: 3, // 1 小时内 ≥ 80 个「老钱包」买家（交易过 8 次以上）；交易 ≤ 3 次的算新开钱包
+  maxBundle: 0.15, earlySec: 15,             // 开盘前 15 秒买的、新开钱包、买入数量一模一样的 → 合计持有 > 15% 不打
+  maxTop10: 0.30, maxDev: 0.05,              // 前 10 大 ≤ 30%、部署者 ≤ 5%
+  maxRoundTrip: 0.25,                        // 模拟「买进马上卖」亏损 > 25%（税高／貔貅）不打
+  lpDrop: 0.4, bundleSellPct: 0.01,          // 买了以后：池子少 40% 或捆绑钱包卖超过 1% 总量 → 立刻全卖
+  backX: 2, steps: [[10, 30], [20, 50]], crash: 0.4, crashAfterBack: 0.6,
+  mintAuto: 1, mintUsd: 5, mintDayUsd: 20, mintMinReal: 50, mintWinMin: 60, mintMaxFresh: 15, // Robinhood 铸造项目：1 小时内 ≥ 50 个真人钱包在铸造、新开钱包占比 ≤ 15%、每次铸造 ≤ 5U 才自动铸 // 卖出照 BSC：2 倍卖回本、10 倍卖 30%、20 倍再卖 50%、从最高回落 40%（回本后 60%）全卖
+};
+const NATIVE = '0xEeeeeEeeeEeEeeEeEeEeeEEEeeeeEeeeeeeeEEeE';
+const RPCS = ['https://rpc.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com', 'https://robinhood.drpc.org', 'https://4663.rpc.thirdweb.com']; /* 官方会限流 → 轮流用三个；nodeflare 每 10 秒只给 1 次，而且历史查询会回假资料，不用 */
+const TRANSFER = '0xddf252ad1be2c89b69c2b068fc378daa952ba7f163c4a11628f55a4df523b3ef';
+const ZERO = '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000', DEAD = '0x000000000000000000000000000000000000dead';
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const lc = (x) => String(x || '').toLowerCase();
+const isAddr = (x) => /^0x[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(lc(x));
+
+export function startRH(ctx) {
+  const { WALLETS, log, tg, S, save, radarOff } = ctx;
+  S.rh = S.rh || {}; S.rh.pos = S.rh.pos || {}; S.rh.seen = S.rh.seen || {}; S.rh.black = S.rh.black || {}; S.rh.day = S.rh.day || {};
+  const R = S.rh; let ri = 0;
+  async function rpc(method, params) { let last; for (let i = 0; i < RPCS.length; i++) { const u = RPCS[(ri + i) % RPCS.length]; try { const rs = await fetch(u, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 1, method, params }), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20000) }); if (rs.status === 429) throw new Error('Too Many Requests'); const j = await rs.json(); if (j.error) throw new Error(j.error.message); return j.result; } catch (e) { last = e; ri++; if (/Too Many|rate/i.test(e.message)) await sleep(800); } } throw last; }
+  async function batch(calls) { const out = []; for (let i = 0; i < calls.length; i += 20) { const part = calls.slice(i, i + 20); let ok = false; for (let k = 0; k < RPCS.length && !ok; k++) { try { const j = await (await fetch(RPCS[(ri + k) % RPCS.length], { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(part.map(([m, p], n) => ({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: n, method: m, params: p }))), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20000) })).json(); const by = new Map((Array.isArray(j) ? j : []).map((x) => [x.id, x.result])); part.forEach((_, n) => out.push(by.get(n) ?? null)); ok = Array.isArray(j); if (!ok) throw 0; } catch { await sleep(600); } } if (!ok) part.forEach(() => out.push(null)); await sleep(300); } return out; }
+  const call = (to, data) => rpc('eth_call', [{ to, data }, 'latest']).catch(() => null);
+  const big = (h) => (h && h !== '0x' ? BigInt(h.slice(0, 66)) : 0n);
+  const wa = (a) => lc(a).replace(/^0x/, '').padStart(64, '0');
+  const day = () => new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 3600e3).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+  const spent = (a) => R.day[day()]?.[a] || 0;
+  const addSpent = (a, u) => { const d = day(); R.day = { [d]: { ...(R.day[d] || {}), [a]: spent(a) + u } }; };
+  let bps = 4; // 每秒几个区块（启动时校正）
+  (async () => { try { const h = parseInt(await rpc('eth_blockNumber', []), 16); const [a, b] = await Promise.all([rpc('eth_getBlockByNumber', ['0x' + h.toString(16), false]), rpc('eth_getBlockByNumber', ['0x' + (h - 20000).toString(16), false])]); const dt = parseInt(a.timestamp, 16) - parseInt(b.timestamp, 16); if (dt > 0) bps = 20000 / dt; log(`[Robinhood] 区块速度约 ${bps.toFixed(2)} 块/秒`); } catch (e) { log('[Robinhood] 校正区块速度失败 ' + e.message); } })();
+
+  async function ethUsd() { try { const j = await (await fetch('https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/search?q=WETH%20USDG', { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000) })).json(); for (const p of j.pairs || []) if (p.chainId === 'robinhood' && p.baseToken?.symbol === 'WETH' && +p.priceUsd > 500) return +p.priceUsd; } catch {} try { const j = await (await fetch('https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/search?q=WETH', { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000) })).json(); for (const p of j.pairs || []) if (p.baseToken?.symbol === 'WETH' && +p.priceUsd > 500) return +p.priceUsd; } catch {} return 0; }
+  async function dsInfo(addrs) { const m = new Map(); for (let i = 0; i < addrs.length; i += 30) { try { const j = await (await fetch(`https://api.dexscreener.com/tokens/v1/robinhood/${addrs.slice(i, i + 30).join(',')}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000) })).json(); for (const p of j || []) { const t = lc(p.baseToken?.address); const liq = +(p.liquidity?.usd) || 0; if (!m.has(t) || liq > m.get(t).liq) m.set(t, { price: +p.priceUsd || 0, liq, pair: lc(p.pairAddress), sym: p.baseToken?.symbol }); } } catch {} } return m; }
+  async function quote(W, from, to, qty) { const r = await W.baw(['market-order', 'quote', '--binanceChainId', '4663', '--fromTokenQty', String(qty), '--fromToken', from, '--toToken', to]); if (!r.j?.success) throw new Error(String(r.j?.error?.message || r.t).slice(0, 120)); return +r.j.data.toCoinAmount; }
+  async function decimals(t) { const d = await call(t, '0x313ce567'); return d ? Number(big(d)) : 18; }
+
+  // ── 候选：GeckoTerminal 热门＋新池（DexScreener 补），同名多池直接跳过 ──
+  async function candidates() {
+    const pools = [];
+    for (const k of ['new_pools', 'trending_pools']) { try { const j = await (await fetch(`https://api.geckoterminal.com/api/v2/networks/robinhood/${k}`, { headers: { accept: 'application/json' }, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000) })).json(); for (const p of j.data || []) { const a = p.attributes || {}; pools.push({ c: lc(String(p.relationships?.base_token?.data?.id || '').split('_').pop()), sym: String(a.name || '').split(' / ')[0].trim(), pool: lc(a.address), liq: +a.reserve_in_usd || 0, ageMin: a.pool_created_at ? (Date.now() - Date.parse(a.pool_created_at)) / 60e3 : 1e9, buys: a.transactions?.h1?.buys || 0, sells: a.transactions?.h1?.sells || 0 }); } } catch {} await sleep(1500); }
+    if (pools.length < 5) { try { const seen = new Set(); for (const u of ['token-profiles/latest/v1', 'token-boosts/latest/v1']) { const j = await (await fetch('https://api.dexscreener.com/' + u, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000) })).json(); for (const x of j || []) if (x.chainId === 'robinhood') seen.add(lc(x.tokenAddress)); }
+        const ids = [...seen].filter(isAddr).slice(0, 30); if (ids.length) { const ps = await (await fetch(`https://api.dexscreener.com/tokens/v1/robinhood/${ids.join(',')}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000) })).json(); for (const p of ps || []) pools.push({ c: lc(p.baseToken?.address), sym: String(p.baseToken?.symbol || ''), pool: lc(p.pairAddress), liq: +(p.liquidity?.usd) || 0, ageMin: p.pairCreatedAt ? (Date.now() - p.pairCreatedAt) / 60e3 : 1e9, buys: p.txns?.h1?.buys || 0, sells: p.txns?.h1?.sells || 0 }); } } catch {} } /* GeckoTerminal 被限流时改用 DexScreener，不会整个瞎掉 */
+    const bySym = new Map(); for (const p of pools) { const s = p.sym.toUpperCase(); if (!bySym.has(s)) bySym.set(s, new Set()); bySym.get(s).add(p.c); }
+    const seen = new Set(), out = [];

+  function note(p, why, retry) { const o = R.seen[p.c] || {}; R.seen[p.c] = { ...o, sym: p.sym || o.sym, at: Date.now(), why, ok: false, retry: !!retry, liq: p.liq ?? o.liq }; }

+  // ── 深度检查：真人买家、捆绑、持币集中、后门、卖得掉 ──
+  async function analyze(c, poolHint, manual, opt = {}) {
+    const head = opt.toBlock || parseInt(await rpc('eth_blockNumber', []), 16); const TB = '0x' + head.toString(16);

+    for (let tries = 0; tries < 3; tries++) { logs = await rpc('eth_getLogs', [{ fromBlock: '0x' + from.toString(16), toBlock: TB, address: c, topics: [TRANSFER] }]); if (logs.some((l) => lc('0x' + l.topics[1].slice(26)) === ZERO) || from === 0) break; from = Math.max(0, from - Math.ceil(bps * 3600 * 24)); }

+    const tf = logs.map((l) => ({ from: lc('0x' + l.topics[1].slice(26)), to: lc('0x' + l.topics[2].slice(26)), v: big(l.data), b: parseInt(l.blockNumber, 16), tx: l.transactionHash }));

+    const cnt = new Map(); for (const t of tf) if (t.from !== ZERO) cnt.set(t.from, (cnt.get(t.from) || new Set()).add(t.tx));
+    let pool = isAddr(poolHint) ? lc(poolHint) : null; if (!pool || !cnt.has(pool)) pool = [...cnt.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1].size - a[1].size)[0]?.[0];

+      else if (pn > 0n) { const [who, v] = others.sort((a, b) => (a[1] > b[1] ? 1 : -1))[0] || []; if (who && v < 0n) sells.push({ who, v: -v, b: arr[0].b }); } }

+    buys.sort((a, b) => a.b - b.b); const first = buys[0].b, earlyEnd = first + Math.max(3, Math.ceil(bps * RHC.earlySec));
+    const early = new Set(buys.filter((x) => x.b <= earlyEnd).map((x) => x.who));
+    const share = (set) => Number(([...set].reduce((s, a) => s + ((bal.get(a) || 0n) > 0n ? bal.get(a) : 0n), 0n) * 10000n) / supply) / 10000;
+    const skipA = new Set([pool, ZERO, DEAD, c]);
+    const holders = [...bal.entries()].filter(([a, v]) => v > 0n && !skipA.has(a)).sort((a, b) => (b[1] > a[1] ? 1 : -1));

+  async function ethBal(a) { return Number(big(await rpc('eth_getBalance', [a, 'latest']).catch(() => '0x0'))) / 1e18; }

+  async function buy(c, sym, facts, manual, usdIn) { const USD = Number(usdIn) > 0 ? Number(usdIn) : RHC.usd;

+  // ── 持仓监控：每 15 秒 ──
+  let monBusy = false;
+  async function monitor() {
+    if (monBusy) return; monBusy = true; try {
+      const cs = Object.keys(R.pos); if (!cs.length) return; const info = await dsInfo(cs); const head = parseInt(await rpc('eth_blockNumber', []), 16);
+      for (const c of cs) { const P = R.pos[c]; const I = info.get(c); let dump = '';

+  // ── Robinhood 铸造项目（11/2）：扫链上「从 0 地址转出」= 铸造，很多不同的真人钱包自己付钱铸同一个新合约 → 列为铸造项目 ──

+  async function scanMints() {

+      { let k = 0; for (const [c, M] of Object.entries(R.mint)) { if (M.name !== undefined || Object.keys(M.m).length < 10) continue; if (++k > 25) break; M.name = strOf(await call(c, '0x06fdde03')).slice(0, 40); M.sym = strOf(await call(c, '0x95d89b41')).slice(0, 16); } } /* 面板上先显示名字，不用等深度检查 */
+      const cands = Object.entries(R.mint).map(([c, M]) => [c, M, Object.values(M.m).filter((t) => Date.now() - t < RHC.mintWinMin * 60e3).length]).filter((x) => x[2] >= 10).sort((a, b) => b[2] - a[2]).slice(0, 4);
+      for (const [c, M, recent] of cands) if (!M.chkAt || Date.now() - M.chkAt > 5 * 60e3) await checkMint(c, M, recent).catch((e) => log('[Robinhood 铸造] 检查出错 ' + e.message));
+      save();
+    } catch (e) { log('[Robinhood 铸造] 扫描出错 ' + e.message); } finally { mintBusy = false; }
+  }
+  async function checkMint(c, M, recent) {
+    M.chkAt = Date.now();
+    if (!M.name && M.name !== '') { M.name = strOf(await call(c, '0x06fdde03')).slice(0, 40); M.sym = strOf(await call(c, '0x95d89b41')).slice(0, 16); }
+    if (/position|wrapped|vault|\blp\b|staked|receipt/i.test(M.name + ' ' + M.sym)) { M.why = '不是铸造项目（看起来是仓位／金库凭证）'; M.ok = false; return; }
+    const minters = Object.entries(M.m).filter(([, t]) => Date.now() - t < RHC.mintWinMin * 60e3).map(([a]) => a).slice(-300);
+    const res = await batch(minters.flatMap((a) => [['eth_getTransactionCount', [a, 'latest']], ['eth_getBalance', [a, 'latest']]]));
+    let real = 0, fresh = 0; minters.forEach((a, i) => { const n = res[2 * i] == null ? -1 : parseInt(res[2 * i], 16), e = res[2 * i + 1] == null ? -1 : Number(big(res[2 * i + 1])) / 1e18; if (n >= RHC.oldNonce && e >= RHC.minEth) real++; if (n >= 0 && n <= RHC.freshNonce) fresh++; });
+    M.real = real; M.recent = recent; M.freshPct = minters.length ? fresh / minters.length * 100 : 0;
+    // 铸造方式：找一笔「自己付钱铸给自己」的交易当范本
+    M.tpl = null; for (const h of [...M.tx].reverse()) { const tx = await rpc('eth_getTransactionByHash', [h]).catch(() => null); if (!tx || !tx.to) continue; const from = lc(tx.from); if (!M.m[from]) continue; const rc = await rpc('eth_getTransactionReceipt', [h]).catch(() => null); const q = (rc?.logs || []).filter((l) => lc(l.address) === c && l.topics[0] === TRANSFER && l.topics[1] === '0x' + '0'.repeat(64) && lc('0x' + l.topics[2].slice(26)) === from).length || 1;
+      M.tpl = { to: lc(tx.to), data: tx.input, value: BigInt(tx.value).toString(), from, q, viaOther: lc(tx.to) !== c }; break; }
+    const px = await ethUsd(); M.priceUsd = M.tpl && px > 0 ? Number(BigInt(M.tpl.value)) / 1e18 * px / (M.tpl.q || 1) : null; M.costUsd = M.tpl && px > 0 ? Number(BigInt(M.tpl.value)) / 1e18 * px : null;
+    const why = []; if (!M.tpl) why.push('找不到一般人自己铸造的交易（可能要白名单或签名）');
+    if (real < RHC.mintMinReal) why.push(`${RHC.mintWinMin} 分钟内真人钱包 ${real} 个（要 ${RHC.mintMinReal} 个）`);
+    if (M.freshPct > RHC.mintMaxFresh) why.push(`新开钱包占 ${M.freshPct.toFixed(0)}%（上限 ${RHC.mintMaxFresh}%，可能是项目方自己刷）`);
+    if (M.costUsd != null && M.costUsd > RHC.mintUsd) why.push(`一次铸造要约 ${M.costUsd.toFixed(1)}U（超过设定 ${RHC.mintUsd}U）`);
+    const cs = globalThis.CUSTOM_SKIP?.({ chain: 'robinhood', type: 'mint', c, name: M.name, sym: M.sym, realBuyers: real }); if (cs) why.push('自订规则：' + cs);
+    M.ok = !why.length; M.why = why.join('；') || '符合条件';
+    if (M.ok && RHC.mintAuto && RHC.auto !== 0 && !radarOff() && !M.done) await doMint(c, M, false);
+  }
+  async function doMint(c, M, manual, capU = 0) {
+    if (!M.tpl) return '没有可以照着做的铸造方式';
+    const px = await ethUsd(); const out = []; M.done = M.done || {};
+    for (const W of WALLETS) { if (M.done[W.addr]) continue;
+      const dd = day(); const sp = (R.mintDay[dd] || {})[W.addr] || 0; if (!manual && sp + (M.costUsd || 0) > RHC.mintDayUsd) { out.push(`[${W.name}] 今天铸造额度用完`); continue; }
+      const data = String(M.tpl.data).toLowerCase().split(M.tpl.from.slice(2)).join(W.addr.slice(2)); // 范本里如果写了对方的地址，换成我们自己的
+      const val = BigInt(M.tpl.value); if (await ethBal(W.addr) < Number(val) / 1e18 + 0.0003) { out.push(`[${W.name}] Robinhood 上的 ETH 不够`); continue; }
+      const p = await W.baw(['contract-call', 'preview', '--binanceChainId', '4663', '--from', W.addr, '--to', M.tpl.to, '--value', val.toString(), '--inputData', data]);
+      const sim = p.j?.data?.simulationResult, rid = p.j?.data?.requestId; if (!p.j?.success || !rid) { out.push(`[${W.name}] 预演失败：${String(p.j?.error?.message || p.t).slice(0, 80)}`); continue; }
+      const got = (sim?.balanceChanges || []).some((b) => lc(b.owner) === W.addr && lc(b.contractAddress) === c && Number(b.change) > 0) || (sim?.nftChanges || sim?.erc721Changes || []).length > 0;
+      const ethOut = (sim?.balanceChanges || []).filter((b) => lc(b.owner) === W.addr && lc(b.contractAddress).startsWith('0xeeee') && Number(b.change) < 0).reduce((a, b) => a - Number(b.change), 0);
+      if (sim?.allowanceChanges?.length || sim?.authorityChanges?.length) { out.push(`[${W.name}] ⛔ 预演发现会授权或交出钱包权限，不铸（防盗）`); log(`[Robinhood 铸造] ${M.name || c} 预演有授权／权限变化，挡下`); continue; } /* 11/2 BDOG 被盗事件：有授权就不铸，手动也一样 */
+      if ((sim?.balanceChanges || []).some((b) => lc(b.owner) === W.addr && !lc(b.contractAddress).startsWith('0xeeee') && Number(b.change) < 0)) { out.push(`[${W.name}] ⛔ 预演显示会扣你别的币，不铸`); continue; }
+      if (!manual && !got) { out.push(`[${W.name}] 预演看不到我们会拿到东西，不铸`); continue; }
+      if (px > 0 && ethOut * px > (capU > 0 ? capU : Math.max(RHC.mintUsd, M.costUsd || 0) * 1.1 + 0.5)) { out.push(`[${W.name}] 预演要花 ${(ethOut * px).toFixed(1)}U，超过${capU > 0 ? '你这次填的 ' + capU + 'U' : '预期'}，不铸`); continue; }
+      const x = await W.baw(['contract-call', 'execute', '--requestId', rid]); log(`[Robinhood 铸造][${W.name}] ${M.name || c}：${String(x.t).slice(0, 200)}`);
+      if (!x.j?.success) { out.push(`[${W.name}] 送出失败：${String(x.j?.error?.message || x.t).slice(0, 80)}`); continue; }
+      M.done[W.addr] = Date.now(); const cu = px > 0 ? Number(val) / 1e18 * px : 0; R.mintDay = { [dd]: { ...(R.mintDay[dd] || {}), [W.addr]: sp + cu } };
+      if (!M.nft) { const P = R.pos[c] || (R.pos[c] = { sym: M.sym || M.name, at: Date.now(), w: {}, liq0: 0, watch: [], lastB: 0, bundleSold: '0' }); const w = P.w[W.addr] || (P.w[W.addr] = { cost: 0, peak: 0, stage: 0 }); w.cost += cu; } // 代币型：开盘后照卖出规则管
+      (R.mintLog = R.mintLog || []).push({ c, sym: M.sym || M.name, w: W.addr, usd: +cu.toFixed(2), nft: M.nft, at: Date.now() }); out.push(`[${W.name}] ✅ 已铸造${cu ? `（约 ${cu.toFixed(2)}U）` : ''}`);
+    }
+    save(); const msg = out.join('\n'); await tg(`💰 [Robinhood] ${manual ? '手动' : '自动'}铸造 ${M.name || ''}（${M.sym || ''}）\n真人钱包 ${M.real} 个｜新开钱包 ${M.freshPct?.toFixed(0)}%\n${msg}\n${c}${M.nft ? '\n（这是 NFT，雷达不会自动卖，要卖请自己到市场挂单）' : ''}`).catch(() => {}); return msg;
+  }
+  async function previewMint(c) { c = lc(c); const M = R.mint[c]; if (!M) return { err: '没看到' }; if (!M.tpl) await checkMint(c, M, 0).catch(() => {}); if (!M.tpl) return { err: '没范本', why: M.why }; const W = WALLETS[0]; const data = String(M.tpl.data).toLowerCase().split(M.tpl.from.slice(2)).join(W.addr.slice(2));
+    const p = await W.baw(['contract-call', 'preview', '--binanceChainId', '4663', '--from', W.addr, '--to', M.tpl.to, '--value', String(M.tpl.value), '--inputData', data]); return { tpl: M.tpl, raw: String(p.t).slice(0, 4000) }; } /* 只预演、不送出（测试用） */
+  async function manualMint(c, capU) { c = lc(c); const M = R.mint[c]; if (!M) return '雷达还没看到这个铸造项目'; if (!M.tpl) await checkMint(c, M, 0).catch(() => {}); return await doMint(c, M, true, Number(capU) || 0); }
+  const t3 = setInterval(() => { scanMints(); }, 60e3); t3.unref?.(); setTimeout(scanMints, 25e3);
+  if (ctx.noLoop) return { analyze, RHC };
+  const PCT = new Set(['maxBundle', 'maxTop10', 'maxDev', 'maxRoundTrip', 'lpDrop', 'bundleSellPct', 'crash', 'crashAfterBack']);
+  const RDEF = { ...RHC };
+  function apply(set, schema) { Object.assign(RHC, RDEF); for (const it of schema || []) { if (!it.k.startsWith('rh.')) continue; const n = it.k.slice(3); let x = set[it.k]; if (x == null || x === '') continue; x = Number(x); if (!Number.isFinite(x)) continue; x = Math.min(it.max, Math.max(it.min, x)); RHC[n] = PCT.has(n) ? x / 100 : x; } }
+  const t1 = setInterval(() => { scan(); }, 90e3); const t2 = setInterval(() => { monitor(); }, 15e3); t1.unref?.(); t2.unref?.(); setTimeout(scan, 15e3);
+  log('[Robinhood] 自动打启动');
+  async function manualBuy(c, usd) { c = lc(c); const r = await analyze(c, null, true).catch((e) => ({ ok: false, why: e.message })); if (!r.ok) return '❌ 挡下了：' + r.why; const I = (await dsInfo([c])).get(c); r.facts.liq0 = I?.liq || 0; return await buy(c, I?.sym || c.slice(0, 8), r.facts, true, usd); }
+  async function manualSell(c, pct = 100, only = '') { c = lc(c); pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, Number(pct) || 100)); const P = R.pos[c]; if (!P) return '没有这个 Robinhood 持仓'; const out = []; for (const W of WALLETS) { if (only && lc(only) !== W.addr) continue; const w = P.w[W.addr]; if (!w || w.closed) continue; const ok = await sellPct(c, P, W, pct, `你在雷达按了卖 ${pct}%`); if (ok && pct >= 100) w.closed = 1; out.push(`[${W.name}] ${ok ? '✅ 已卖' : '❌ 没卖成'}`); } if (Object.values(P.w).every((w) => w.closed)) { R.done = R.done || {}; R.done[c] = { ...P, endAt: Date.now() }; delete R.pos[c]; } save(); return out.join('\n') || '没有可卖的'; }

+}

diff --git a/smart.mjs b/smart.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c7702d3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/smart.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+// 罗宾猫雷达 · 聪明钱包（11/2）：用户标记的钱包（做市商、大户）＋自动找到的「相关钱包」，只要对某个币有动作就通知
+// 全部本地跑：直接查链上转账纪录（BSC、Robinhood Chain），不用任何 AI、不花钱
+const T = '0xddf252ad1be2c89b69c2b068fc378daa952ba7f163c4a11628f55a4df523b3ef';
+const SKIP = new Set(['0xbb4cdb9cbd36b01bd1cbaebf2de08d9173bc095c', '0x55d398326f99059ff775485246999027b3197955', '0x8ac76a51cc950d9822d68b83fe1ad97b32cd580d', '0xe9e7cea3dedca5984780bafc599bd69add087d56', '0xc5f0f7b66764f6ec8c8dff7ba683102295e16409', '0x8d0d000ee44948fc98c9b98a4fa4921476f08b0d', '0x0bd7d308f8e1639fab988df18a8011f41eacad73']); // WBNB、稳定币、Robinhood WETH：不算「对项目动作」
+const FUND = new Set(['0x55d398326f99059ff775485246999027b3197955', '0x8ac76a51cc950d9822d68b83fe1ad97b32cd580d', '0xbb4cdb9cbd36b01bd1cbaebf2de08d9173bc095c', '0x0bd7d308f8e1639fab988df18a8011f41eacad73']);
+const lc = (x) => String(x || '').toLowerCase(); const pad = (a) => '0x' + lc(a).slice(2).padStart(64, '0');
+export function startSmart({ rpc, log, tg, S, save, radarSet }) {
+  const RH = 'https://rpc.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com';
+  const rhRpc = async (m, p) => { const j = await (await fetch(RH, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 1, method: m, params: p }), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20000) })).json(); if (j.error) throw new Error(j.error.message); return j.result; };
+  const CH = { bsc: { rpc, step: 1200, name: 'BSC' }, robinhood: { rpc: rhRpc, step: 6000, name: 'Robinhood' } };
+  S.smartW = S.smartW || {}; const Z = S.smartW; Z.last = Z.last || {}; Z.related = Z.related || {}; Z.seen = Z.seen || {}; Z.events = Z.events || []; /* 用自己的栏位，不跟旧资料打架 */
+  const list = () => { const o = {}; for (const x of Array.isArray(radarSet()['smart.wallets']) ? radarSet()['smart.wallets'] : []) { const a = lc(x?.addr); if (/^0x[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(a)) o[a] = String(x.label || '聪明钱包').slice(0, 30); } if (radarSet()['smart.related'] !== 0) for (const [a, r] of Object.entries(Z.related)) if (!o[a] && o[r.of]) o[a] = (o[r.of] || '') + ' 的相关钱包'; return o; };
+  const symCache = {};
+  async function symOf(ch, c) { const k = ch + c; if (symCache[k]) return symCache[k]; try { const h = await CH[ch].rpc('eth_call', [{ to: c, data: '0x95d89b41' }, 'latest']); const b = Buffer.from(h.slice(2), 'hex'); const n = b.length >= 96 ? Number(BigInt('0x' + b.slice(32, 64).toString('hex'))) : 0; symCache[k] = (n ? b.slice(64, 64 + Math.min(n, 20)).toString('utf8') : b.toString('utf8')).replace(/\0/g, '').trim() || c.slice(0, 8); } catch { symCache[k] = c.slice(0, 8); } return symCache[k]; }
+  let busy = false;
+  async function tick() {
+    if (busy) return; busy = true; try {
+      const L = list(); const addrs = Object.keys(L); if (!addrs.length) return;
+      for (const [ch, C] of Object.entries(CH)) {
+        let head; try { head = parseInt(await C.rpc('eth_blockNumber', []), 16); } catch { continue; }
+        let from = Z.last[ch] ? Z.last[ch] + 1 : head - 200; if (head - from > C.step) from = head - C.step; if (from > head) continue;
+        const q = (topics) => C.rpc('eth_getLogs', [{ fromBlock: '0x' + from.toString(16), toBlock: '0x' + head.toString(16), topics }]).catch(() => null);
+        const padded = addrs.slice(0, 200).map(pad); const outL = await q([T, padded]); const inL = await q([T, null, padded]);
+        if (outL == null && inL == null) continue; Z.last[ch] = head;
+        const acts = {};
+        for (const [l, dir] of [...(inL || []).map((l) => [l, '买']), ...(outL || []).map((l) => [l, '卖'])]) {
+          const tok = lc(l.address); const who = lc('0x' + (dir === '买' ? l.topics[2] : l.topics[1]).slice(26)); const other = lc('0x' + (dir === '买' ? l.topics[1] : l.topics[2]).slice(26));
+          if (!L[who]) continue;
+          if (dir === '卖' && FUND.has(tok) && !L[other] && other !== '0x' + '0'.repeat(40)) { // 聪明钱包把钱转给别人 → 可能是它的小号
+            const code = await C.rpc('eth_getCode', [other, 'latest']).catch(() => '0x'); if (code === '0x' && !Z.related[other] && Object.keys(Z.related).length < 100) { Z.related[other] = { of: Z.related[who]?.of || who, at: Date.now(), ch }; log(`[聪明钱包] 找到相关钱包 ${other}（${L[who]} 转钱给它）`); } }
+          if (SKIP.has(tok)) continue;
+          const k = ch + '|' + tok + '|' + dir; (acts[k] = acts[k] || { ch, tok, dir, who: new Set(), n: 0 }).who.add(who); acts[k].n++;
+        }
+        for (const A of Object.values(acts)) {
+          const key = A.ch + A.tok + A.dir; const prev = Z.seen[key]; if (prev && Date.now() - prev < 30 * 60e3) continue; Z.seen[key] = Date.now(); /* 同一个币同方向 30 分钟只通知一次 */
+          const sym = await symOf(A.ch, A.tok); const names = [...A.who].map((a) => L[a]).slice(0, 3).join('、');
+          const ev = { at: Date.now(), ch: A.ch, tok: A.tok, sym, dir: A.dir, who: [...A.who], names }; Z.events.push(ev); if (Z.events.length > 300) Z.events.splice(0, Z.events.length - 300);
+          await tg(`🦋 聪明钱包${A.dir === '买' ? '在买' : '在卖'}：${sym}（${CH[A.ch].name}）\n${names}${A.who.size > 1 ? ` 等 ${A.who.size} 个钱包` : ''}｜${A.n} 笔\n${A.tok}`).catch(() => {});
+        }
+      }
+      for (const [k, t] of Object.entries(Z.seen)) if (Date.now() - t > 6 * 3600e3) delete Z.seen[k];
+      save();
+    } catch (e) { log('[聪明钱包] 出错 ' + e.message); } finally { busy = false; }
+  }
+  setInterval(tick, 30e3).unref(); setTimeout(tick, 20e3); log('[聪明钱包] 监控启动');
+}

+async function agTokScan() {
+  if (radarOff() || Number(radarSet()['bsc.tokAuto']) === 0) return; /* 11/2：BSC 热门代币有自己的开关 */

         else if (/^(help|指令|說明|说明)$/i.test(txt)) await tgSay('可用指令：\n賣 XXX → 所有錢包全賣\n賣 XXX 一半 → 各賣一半\n（也可打「XXX 全出」「XXX 出一半」）\n餘額 → 看各錢包 BNB\n持倉 → 還沒賣的幣和現值\n戰績 → 上線以來打了多少、賣了多少');

diff --git "a/\345\256\232\346\212\225.mjs" "b/\345\256\232\346\212\225.mjs"
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1e0d036
--- /dev/null
+++ "b/\345\256\232\346\212\225.mjs"
@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+// 定投 DCA（官方范本）：每隔固定时间买一点，不猜高低，摊平成本。
+// 本金是钱包里的 USDT；只会买，不会自动卖（要卖用网格或自己手动）。
+export const meta = {
+  id: 'dca', name: '定投', nameEn: 'DCA', everySec: 300, dayUsd: 20,
+  desc: '每隔一段时间固定买一点大币，摊平成本。', descEn: 'Buy a fixed amount of a blue chip on a schedule.',
+  settings: [
+    { k: 'coin', def: 0, min: 0, max: 1, zh: '币种（0=BNB，1=ETH）', en: 'Coin (0=BNB, 1=ETH)', dzh: '只开放大币', den: 'Blue chips only' },
+    { k: 'everyH', def: 24, min: 1, max: 720, unit: '小时', uen: 'h', zh: '每隔几小时买一次', en: 'Every (hours)', dzh: '', den: '' },
+    { k: 'usd', def: 5, min: 1, max: 1e9, unit: 'U', zh: '每次买多少', en: 'Amount', dzh: '', den: '' },
+  ],
+};
+export async function tick(api) {
+  const c = api.settings(); const coin = c.coin >= 1 ? 'ETH' : 'BNB'; const s = api.state;
+  if (s.last && Date.now() - s.last < c.everyH * 3600e3) return;
+  const r = await api.buy(coin, c.usd); if (!r.ok) { if (!s.warned || Date.now() - s.warned > 6 * 3600e3) { s.warned = Date.now(); api.log('定投没买成：' + r.why); } return; }
+  s.last = Date.now(); api.notify(`定投买进 ${coin} ${c.usd}U`);
+}
diff --git "a/\347\275\221\346\240\274.mjs" "b/\347\275\221\346\240\274.mjs"
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9ec0f11
--- /dev/null
+++ "b/\347\275\221\346\240\274.mjs"
@@ -0,0 +1,32 @@
+// 网格交易（官方范本）：价格在区间内每跌一格买一份、每涨一格卖一份，赚来回的价差。
+// 只适合 BNB、ETH 这种大币；土狗不要用网格（一天跌 90% 会整个套住）。
+// 本金是钱包里的 USDT；只卖网格自己买的部位，碰不到雷达打到的币。
+export const meta = {
+  id: 'grid', name: '网格交易', nameEn: 'Grid trading', everySec: 60, dayUsd: 50,
+  desc: '在价格区间内低买高卖。只用于大币，本金是 USDT。', descEn: 'Buy low / sell high inside a range. Blue chips only; funded with USDT.',
+  settings: [
+    { k: 'coin', def: 0, min: 0, max: 1, zh: '币种（0=BNB，1=ETH）', en: 'Coin (0=BNB, 1=ETH)', dzh: '只开放大币', den: 'Blue chips only' },
+    { k: 'low', def: 0, min: 0, max: 100000, unit: 'U', zh: '区间下限', en: 'Range low', dzh: '0 = 自动（开始时的现价往下 15%）。跌破就不再买', den: '0 = auto (start price −15%). No buys below' },
+    { k: 'high', def: 0, min: 0, max: 100000, unit: 'U', zh: '区间上限', en: 'Range high', dzh: '0 = 自动（开始时的现价往上 15%）。涨破就不再卖', den: '0 = auto (start price +15%). No sells above' },
+    { k: 'levels', def: 10, min: 2, max: 50, unit: '格', uen: 'levels', zh: '分几格', en: 'Levels', dzh: '格子越多，交易越频繁、每次赚越少', den: 'More levels = more, smaller trades' },
+    { k: 'perUsd', def: 5, min: 1, max: 1e9, unit: 'U', zh: '每格买多少', en: 'Per level', dzh: '每跌一格买进的金额', den: 'Buy size per level' },
+  ],
+};
+export async function tick(api) {
+  const c = api.settings(); const coin = c.coin >= 1 ? 'ETH' : 'BNB';
+  const px = await api.price(coin); const s0 = api.state;
+  if (s0.coin !== coin || !(s0.autoLow > 0)) { s0.autoLow = px * 0.85; s0.autoHigh = px * 1.15; }
+  const low = c.low > 0 ? c.low : s0.autoLow, high = c.high > 0 ? c.high : s0.autoHigh; c.low = low; c.high = high;
+  if (!(c.high > c.low)) return api.log('区间设定不对：上限要大于下限');
+  const step = (c.high - c.low) / c.levels;
+  const lv = Math.max(0, Math.min(c.levels, Math.floor((px - c.low) / step)));
+  const s = api.state; if (s.coin !== coin) { s.coin = coin; s.lv = lv; s.lots = []; api.log(`开始：${coin} 现价 ${px.toFixed(2)}，区间 ${c.low.toFixed(2)}～${c.high.toFixed(2)}，在第 ${lv} 格`); api.notify(`网格开始：${coin} 区间 ${c.low.toFixed(0)}～${c.high.toFixed(0)}，分 ${c.levels} 格，每格 ${c.perUsd}U`); return; }
+  if (px < c.low || px > c.high) return; // 区间外不动作
+  if (lv < s.lv) { // 跌了 → 每跌一格买一份
+    for (let i = 0; i < s.lv - lv; i++) { const r = await api.buy(coin, c.perUsd); if (!r.ok) { api.log('买不了：' + r.why); break; } s.lots.push({ qty: r.qty, at: px }); api.notify(`${coin} 跌到 ${px.toFixed(2)}，买进 ${c.perUsd}U`); }
+  } else if (lv > s.lv) { // 涨了 → 每涨一格卖一份（先卖最便宜买的）
+    s.lots.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at);
+    for (let i = 0; i < lv - s.lv && s.lots.length; i++) { const lot = s.lots[0]; if (px < lot.at + step * 0.9) break; const r = await api.sell(coin, lot.qty); if (!r.ok) { api.log('卖不了：' + r.why); break; } s.lots.shift(); api.notify(`${coin} 涨到 ${px.toFixed(2)}，卖出一份，回收 ${r.usd.toFixed(2)}U`); }
+  }
+  s.lv = lv;
+}
diff --git "a/\351\207\215\345\220\257\347\275\227\345\256\276\347\214\253\351\233\267\350\276\276.vbs" "b/\351\207\215\345\220\257\347\275\227\345\256\276\347\214\253\351\233\267\350\276\276.vbs"

+sh.Run "taskkill /IM electron.exe /F", 0, True
+WScript.Sleep 2500
+d = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").GetParentFolderName(WScript.ScriptFullName)
+sh.CurrentDirectory = d
+sh.Run """" & d & "\node_modules\electron\dist\electron.exe"" """ & d & """", 1, False
